### [1] [Who Should Own the Expert Cache? Kernel-Managed Tiering for Trillion-Parameter MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12103)
*Yuan Si,Yufeng Lin,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，在混合专家模型(MoE)服务中，直接使用操作系统内核的页缓存管理专家缓存，性能接近甚至等同于经过精细调优的用户空间频率排序缓存方案。因此建议在万亿参数级模型服务中，让内核管理缓存淘汰，模型特定知识用于缓存准入和建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合专家模型(MoE)服务系统因专家池规模远超DRAM容量，必须引入缓存，通常采用用户空间实现的、基于频率排序的、显式固定多级缓存方案。研究者希望探索操作系统内核自带的页缓存机制是否可以作为替代方案，以简化系统设计并降低管理复杂度。

Method: 作者使用三个MoE模型(每个层128到896个专家不等，包含一个万亿参数生产模型，专家池达1.45TB)的路由器追踪数据，在GH200节点上原生回放完整专家池，通过三种独立机制强制执行容量限制。系统性地评估了内核页缓存作为专家缓存层的性能表现，并与用户空间频率排序缓存方案进行对比，同时测试了路由器预取机制的效果。

Result: 研究发现四个主要结论：1)迭代时间和设备流量是缓存容量的平滑可复现函数，使DRAM成为万亿参数模型服务实用的调节旋钮；2)深度压力拐点是内存回收伪像，需要同时使用MGLRU和气球式大部分锁定内存；基于气球的研究可能将压力端设备流量夸大约2倍；3)在等内存墙约束下，未调优的内核LRU缓存命中率(75.3%)与同域Oracle频率表(74.6%)基本持平，Oracle的机制优势仅为1.09倍且在异域场景消失；4)路由器预取(64.7%召回率)作为内核预读建议仅带来0.3%收益，作为同步预取无收益。端到端启用内核缓存管理可将解码性能提升1.09-1.10倍。

Conclusion: 研究确立了明确的设计原则：在万亿参数级MoE模型服务场景下，应让操作系统内核管理缓存淘汰决策，而将模型特定知识用于缓存准入和I/O建议。这种方案简化了系统架构，同时保持了与精细调优方案相当的性能，为大规模MoE模型的高效服务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts models whose expert pools dwarf DRAM force every serving system to contain a cache, yet existing systems typically implement this cache in user space using expert-granular, frequency-ranked, explicitly pinned tiers. We characterize the alternative already provided by the OS: the page cache as the expert tier. We use router traces from three MoE models spanning 128 to 896 experts per layer, including a production trillion-parameter model with a 1.45 TB expert pool, and replay them natively against the full pool on GH200 nodes with capacity enforced by three independent mechanisms.
  We find four main results. First, iteration time and device traffic are smooth, reproducible functions of cache capacity, making DRAM a practical sizing knob for trillion-parameter serving. Second, the deep-pressure knee is a reclaim artifact requiring both MGLRU and balloon-style mostly-mlocked memory; cgroup limits and physical-memory configurations show no such amplification, indicating that balloon-based studies can overstate pressured-end device traffic by approximately 2$\times$. Third, under an enforced equal-memory wall, untuned kernel LRU serves essentially as much demand as a same-domain oracle frequency table (75.3% versus 74.6% at 256 GB), while the oracle's mechanism advantage is only 1.09$\times$ and disappears off-domain, where LRU remains at 70--71% hit rate. Fourth, router lookahead with 64.7% measured recall provides only a 0.3% benefit as kernel readahead advice and no benefit as synchronous prefetch. End to end, enabling kernel-managed caching improves decode performance by 1.09--1.10$\times$ with token-identical outputs across nine balanced pairs. The resulting design principle is simple: in this regime, let the kernel own eviction, while model-specific knowledge is best spent on admission and advice.

</details>


### [2] [The Ingestion Tax: Adopting File-Backed Weights in Tensor Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12114)
*Yuan Si,Yufeng Lin,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出file-backed weight adoption技术，消除深度学习推理中的"ingestion tax"开销，实现零拷贝权重导入，使页缓存成为可回收的加速器存储层级，在小批量解码场景下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重模型在中等容量场景下存在"ingestion tax"问题：权重文件页已驻留在GPU可访问的内存域，但框架仍需拷贝到框架管理的分配中，导致每个token生成时重复读取权重。现有系统在集成内存和一致内存架构下，OS已持有可回收的文件页，硬件架构支持GPU直接读取，但框架的ownership model成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出file-backed weight adoption机制：(1)框架无关的生产者使用MAP_SHARED映射每个张量；(2)将页包装为无拷贝的GPU缓冲区；(3)导出DLPack胶囊供PyTorch或MLX导入。关键创新是建立三部分执行契约：读取映射、保持激活驻留在加速器、GPU上排序，违反契约会导致性能下降。通过随机因子实验验证契约必要性。

Result: 在契约下，adoption以零拷贝开销消除ingestion tax：带宽从默认构造函数的53-82 GB/s提升至516 GB/s，与驻留存储上的相同内核性能相当（差异[-0.66%, +0.48%]）。Qwen2.5-72B推理速度7.14 vs 7.23 tok/s。多进程解码时，N个进程可共享一个映射副本，而驻留加载需要N倍开销（满载时5.5 vs 0.08 tok/s）。65GB检查点首token延迟降低6.4x，Kimi K3 spine阶段从2.62降至0.35 s/token。在AMD APU上llama.cpp性能提升1.21x且内存占用减半，在GH200上与重叠流式传输相当，但跨PCIe性能下降39x。

Conclusion: 内存拓扑而非API决定字节路径。部署规则应将页缓存视为第一类、可回收的加速器存储层级。该机制在集成内存和一致内存系统上效果显著，但在PCIe连接系统上性能下降明显，证明了内存拓扑对性能的关键影响。

Abstract: Open-weight models can occupy a middle capacity regime: active weights fit in DRAM as cached file pages, but a second framework-owned representation does not fit or must be refilled as layers run, so low-batch decode rereads the weights every token. On integrated and coherent-memory systems the checkpoint's file pages already occupy a GPU-readable domain, yet accelerator-loading paths still copy them into framework-owned allocations. We call this the ingestion tax: the OS holds the bytes as clean, evictable file pages, the architecture makes them GPU-readable, and only the framework's ownership model stands between the two.
  We present file-backed weight adoption: a framework-independent producer maps each tensor with MAP_SHARED, wraps the pages as a no-copy GPU buffer, and exports a DLPack capsule that PyTorch or MLX imports as ordinary storage. Zero-copy import alone is not enough: a randomized factorial establishes a three-part execution contract - read the mapping, keep activations accelerator-resident, order on the GPU; a bolt-on extension forfeiting the last two runs 2.3x slower than stock. Under the contract, adoption removes the tax at no rate cost: 516 GB/s where default constructors reach 53-82, at parity with the same kernel over resident storage ([-0.66%, +0.48%]; Qwen2.5-72B 7.14 vs 7.23 tok/s).
  Parity buys ownership: N processes decode one mapped copy where resident loading pays N (at capacity, 5.5 vs 0.08 tok/s); a 65 GB checkpoint reaches its first token 6.4x sooner; the Kimi K3 spine stage falls from 2.62 to 0.35 s per token, 3.8x from storage alone. The same mechanism improves llama.cpp 1.21x at half the footprint on an AMD APU, ties overlapped streaming on a capacity-exceeding GH200, and loses 39x across PCIe: memory topology, not the API, decides the byte path. The deployment rule treats the page cache as a first-class, reclaimable accelerator-storage tier.

</details>

### [3] [When Do Institutions Beat Intelligence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11357)
*Zhengye Han*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现，在多智能体系统中，更强的个体能力不一定带来更强的集体能力。机构机制能在修复集体构建公共状态失败时发挥作用，但在信号不可靠、智能体能独立完成转换或状态无法支持可靠行动时优势丧失。研究将智能与机构的选择重新定义为对集体推理失败点的诊断问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统面临集体推理失败问题：证据路由不当、不可靠报告进入公共信念、相关声明伪装成独立支持、共享状态过期或被策略性扭曲、有用证据通过无效行动接口暴露等。需要理解何时应通过增强智能体能力来改善推理，何时应通过改变机构结构来解决问题。

Method: 基于群体决策和分布式认知研究的功能区分，围绕集体失败的四个关键点构建受控人工实验环境：访问与路由、准入与依赖、状态维护与激励、表示与行动。在实验中分别变化模型能力和机构结构，将正向干预与匹配的推理基线和机制破坏对照进行配对比较。

Result: 实验揭示了明确的边界条件：当机构能修复集体构建可用公共状态的失败时，它能提供帮助；但当其信号不可知或不可检验、当更强智能体能直接执行相同转换、或当结果状态无法支持可靠行动时，机构优势消失。

Conclusion: 智能与机构之间的选择本质上是对集体推理失败点的诊断问题。研究为多智能体系统设计提供了理论指导，强调了在特定失败模式下选择合适干预策略的重要性。

Abstract: More capable agents do not necessarily form a more capable collective. A multi-agent system may jointly possess sufficient information yet fail because evidence is poorly routed, unreliable reports enter public belief, correlated claims masquerade as independent support, shared state becomes stale or strategically distorted, or useful evidence is exposed through an ineffective action interface. We ask when additional resources should improve the reasoner and when they should instead change the institutional structure through which the collective forms and acts on public information. Drawing on functional distinctions from research on group decision making and distributed cognition, we construct controlled artificial ecologies around four loci of collective failure: access and routing, admission and dependence, state maintenance and incentives, and representation and action. Across these ecologies, we separately vary model capability and institutional structure, pairing positive interventions with matched reasoning baselines and mechanism-breaking controls. The experiments reveal a consistent boundary: institutions help when they repair failures in how a collective constructs usable public state, but lose their advantage when their signals are uninformative or uncheckable, when stronger intelligence can perform the same transformation directly, or when the resulting state cannot support reliable action. Our results recast the choice between intelligence and institutions as a diagnosis of where collective reasoning fails.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond Memory: A Transactional Continuity Kernel for Long-Lived AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11632)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Continuity Kernel (CK)，一个用于持久化AI智能体的状态治理激活合约，通过原子化的激活交易确保状态更新的授权性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化AI智能体在长期运行中积累版本化状态，但仅靠存储保留无法识别权威状态。缺乏显式控制平面时，模型、工具和后台工作者的未中介更新可能导致陈旧覆盖、未审计暴露和自我授权权限升级等风险。

Method: 提出Continuity Kernel (CK)激活合约，将离线提交候选评估与原子状态激活解耦。不可信组件针对精确前驱头或类型化缺失提出类型化变更，通过简短激活交易重新验证所有权、前状态权威性、新鲜度和效果唯一性，记录稳定处置结果（提交、拒绝、隔离或延迟）。仅提交操作原子化推进分支头并安装完整的接受单元。

Result: 在有界可执行模型上验证协议，覆盖2,808,230个可达状态和5,526,474个状态变更转换，实现零不变式违规。

Conclusion: 证明了智能体状态治理作为基础设施激活问题的可行性，通过显式控制平面有效解决了持久化AI智能体状态管理中的权威性、安全性和一致性问题。

Abstract: Persistent AI agents accumulate versioned state across long horizons, but storage retention alone does not identify authoritative state. Without an explicit control plane, unmediated updates by models, tools, and background workers risk stale overwrites, un-audited exposures, and self-authorizing privilege escalation. We argue that agent state governance is an infrastructural activation problem, defining continuity as an unbroken, authorized lineage of accepted branch heads. We present the Continuity Kernel (CK), an activation contract that decouples off-commit candidate evaluation from atomic state activation. Untrusted components propose typed changes against an exact predecessor head or typed absence. A short activation transaction revalidates ownership, pre-state authority, freshness, and effect uniqueness, recording one stable disposition (Commit, Reject, Quarantine, or Defer). Only Commit atomically advances the branch head and installs the complete accepted unit (state, authority, lineage, effects, outcome, and receipt). A bounded executable model verifies the protocol across 2,808,230 reachable states and 5,526,474 state-changing transitions with zero invariant violations.

</details>


### [5] [Rethinking Agent Security as a Networking Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12172)
*Van Tran,Taveesh Sharma,Tajveer Singh Dhesi,Nick Feamster*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对AI代理的隐私安全风险，提出结合确定性执行机制与语义上下文感知策略的系统安全方法，并给出参考架构与研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理能力提升与广泛部署带来生产力提升与应用创新，但其日益增长的自主性引入显著的隐私安全风险。现有防御多为代理中心式，依赖代理自身检测威胁并执行策略，而LLM驱动的代理行为本质上非确定性且易受提示注入等攻击，导致无法可靠防御，亟需新方案。

Method: 借鉴网络社区成熟原则（集中控制分布执行、能力化访问、零信任最小权限），提出系统化安全方法：结合确定性执行机制（强安全保证）与语义上下文感知策略（细粒度决策）。在此基础上提出参考架构，并识别关键研究问题。

Result: 形成一套面向AI代理系统安全的参考架构与研究方向，强调确定性机制与语义感知策略的结合，为安全与隐私保护的AI代理系统设计提供指导。

Conclusion: 仅靠网络安全原则不足以应对AI代理的语义上下文依赖问题。通过融合确定性执行与语义感知策略，可望为AI代理系统提供更强的安全保证，需进一步研究实现细节与落地路径。

Abstract: AI agents are rapidly becoming more capable and widely deployed, promising substantial gains in productivity and enabling new classes of applications. However, their growing autonomy also introduces significant privacy and security risks. Existing defenses are predominantly agent-centric, relying on the agent itself to detect threats and enforce privacy and security policies. This approach is fundamentally limited because it entrusts policy enforcement to AI agents whose LLM-driven behavior is inherently nondeterministic and vulnerable to manipulation through attacks such as prompt injection. As a result, current defenses cannot reliably prevent privacy and security threats, highlighting a critical need for a new solution to securing AI agent systems.
  The networking community has long grappled with similar challenges and offers insightful principles we can borrow to design a more secure AI agent system. These include centralized control with distributed enforcement, capability-based access for mediating requests to sensitive resources, and least privilege through zero-trust enforcement. Historically, these principles have provided strong deterministic guarantees for networked systems. However, these principles alone are insufficient for AI agents because the safety and appropriateness of an agent's actions often depend on semantic context beyond the expressiveness of static rules.
  Building on these principles, we advocate for a systematic approach to AI agent security that combines deterministic enforcement mechanisms, which provide strong security guarantees, with semantic, context-aware policies that enable nuanced decision-making. We then present a reference architecture and identify key research questions and future directions to guide the design of secure and privacy-preserving AI agent systems.

</details>

### [6] [Self-evolving network verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11340)
*Ioannis Protogeros,Tibor Schneider,Laurent Vanbever*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动演化网络验证器模型的方法，通过编码代理和可信预言机的反例引导循环，使符号网络验证器能够自动学习并准确捕捉实际网络行为，无需人工手动编码和维护协议特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的符号网络验证器需要专家手工编写协议和特性编码，但由于厂商实现与RFC规范存在偏差、版本更新导致行为变化，创建和维护准确的控制平面模型既困难又永无止境，这种持续维护负担使得许多需要验证的网络无法使用验证技术。

Method: 提出反例引导的自动化模型演化方法：利用路由器软件本身作为唯一明确的规范来源，在循环中，编码代理向验证器的符号编码提出扩展建议，可信预言机（如模拟路由器）提供真实路由状态作为基准真值，代理根据每次与预言机的差异迭代优化网络模型。

Result: 原型系统成功教会一个3000行基于SMT的验证器学习三个原先不支持的特性：OSPF区域、BGP路由反射和EVPN上的L3VPN，能够自主收敛到与预言机匹配的模型，甚至发现了厂商特定的行为特征。

Conclusion: 自动化模型增长将难题从编写验证系统转变为系统化测试验证系统，作者提出了信任和利用自动演化验证器的研究议程，为网络验证技术的广泛应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Symbolic network verifiers can reason about correctness across vast spaces of routing inputs and failures, but only for the protocols and features an expert has encoded by hand. Creating and maintaining a faithful model of the control plane is both difficult and never-ending, since no written source specifies perfectly what a network does: vendor implementations deviate from the RFCs, and behaviour shifts with releases. The burden of constant upkeep ultimately keeps verification out of many networks that need it.
  We argue that the model should instead evolve automatically to faithfully capture the actual network behaviour. To achieve that, we leverage the only source that specifies it unambiguously: the router software itself. In a counterexample-guided loop, a coding agent proposes extensions to the verifier's symbolic encoding, while a trusted oracle (e.g., emulated routers) supplies the ground-truth routing state. The agent iteratively refines the network model using each disagreement with the oracle.
  As early evidence, a prototype of this system taught a 3,000-line SMT-based verifier three features it did not support: OSPF areas, BGP route reflection, and L3VPN over EVPN, converging autonomously on models that match the oracle, even noticing vendor-specific behaviour. Automating model growth shifts the hard problem from writing verification systems to systematically testing them; we propose a research agenda for trusting and harnessing automatically evolved verifiers.

</details>


### [7] [Multi-Pair Fidelity-Aware Rate Allocation in a Quantum Network: Approximation Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11501)
*Zunzheng Zhang,Xuanli Lin,Zhaofeng Zhang,Nageswara S. V. Rao,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子网络中三种保真度感知的速率分配问题均为NP难，并为相应的优化问题提供了全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中的纠缠分发需要同时考虑有限的链路容量、概率性的纠缠交换以及异构的链路保真度。此前研究仅解决了所有链路保真度相同时的特例，而实际网络链路保真度往往各异，因此需要研究更一般情况下的保真度感知速率分配问题。

Method: 提出了三种速率分配问题：速率和最大化、带最小速率约束的速率和最大化以及最大-最小公平性分配。通过复杂性分析证明这三个问题均为NP难。随后研究这些问题的优化版本，即在满足吞吐量或公平性要求下最大化最小端到端保真度，并为这些优化问题设计了全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。

Result: 证明了三种保真度感知的速率分配问题均为NP难，填补了此前仅知特例情况的复杂性空白；为相应的优化问题提供了理论保证的全多项式时间近似方案；在随机生成的网络上进行实验，验证了所提方案的计算有效性。

Conclusion: 在量子网络中考虑异构链路保真度的多对速率分配问题在计算上是困难的，但可以通过近似算法在实际中有效求解。所提出的FPTAS为量子网络的纠缠分发提供了理论与实用兼顾的解决方案。

Abstract: Entanglement distribution in quantum networks must jointly account for limited link capacities, probabilistic entanglement swapping, and heterogeneous link fidelities. In this paper, we study multi-pair fidelity-aware rate allocation in quantum networks. We formulate three rate-allocation problems: rate sum, rate sum subject to minimum-rate constraints, and max-min fairness. Prior work has studied a special case of the rate sum problem, where all links have identical fidelity. This special case admits a polynomial-time algorithm. We prove that all three problems are NP-hard. We then study optimization versions of these problems which maximize the minimum end-to-end fidelity subject to throughput or fairness requirements. We present fully polynomial-time approximation schemes (FPTAS) for solving these optimization problems. Experiments on randomly generated networks demonstrate the computational effectiveness of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [8] [In QKD, Key Metadata is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11502)
*Alin-Bogdan Popa,Pantelimon George Popescu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦互联QKD网络中跨域密钥分发面临的互操作性问题，提出通过QKD密钥元数据开发通用语言，从句法互操作性转向语义互操作性，并给出6步标准化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着联邦互联QKD网络逐渐成为常态，虽然已有多个标准实现技术层面的互操作性，但跨域密钥分发在安全事件通报与缓解、空间段集成、QKD即服务商业模式、密钥新鲜度与管辖权等方面仍存在未解决问题。当前仅有句法互操作性不足以满足复杂场景需求，需要传递语义信息的通用语言。

Method: 提出通过QKD密钥元数据构建通用语言以实现语义互操作性，并制定包含6个步骤的标准化流程来系统性地开发密钥元数据标准。

Result: 论证了从句法互操作性向语义互操作性转变的可行性，提出了基于密钥元数据的解决方案框架，为跨域密钥分发中的安全、商业、管辖权等问题提供了语义层面的解决思路。

Conclusion: 通过建立QKD密钥元数据标准，能够实现密钥及其语义信息的有效传递，为未来QKD即服务模式和联邦互联QKD网络的实际部署奠定基础。

Abstract: Federated interconnected QKD networks are becoming the norm, and with several standards already in place, interoperability is now technically feasible. Many aspects related to cross-domain key distribution, however, are left unresolved: breach announcement and mitigation, integration with the space segment, extension to a future QKD-as-a-Service commercial model, custom requests referring to key freshness, jurisdictions, security levels, etc. We argue that, to move from syntactic to semantic interoperability, a common language must be developed to ensure transfer of meaning along with the keys. We posit this is possible via QKD key metadata, and we propose a 6-step process to develop it as a standard.

</details>


### [9] [Integrated Sensing and Communication in 3GPP: Evolution from 5G-Advanced to 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11606)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了ISAC在3GPP从5G-Advanced到6G的演进，涵盖Release-19/20标准化内容及6G原生感知技术，为网络级感知提供统一技术视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC将移动网络从信息传输扩展到对被动目标和环境的感知，需要在复用频谱、基础设施和边缘计算的同时，解决多应用接入、异构感知实体、不确定性、隐私信任以及与通信服务集成等挑战。

Method: 综述分析3GPP演进路径：Release-19的ISAC服务需求与信道模型、Release-20的感知功能架构与报告抽象、5G单站基线的无人机检测追踪，以及6G原生感知技术（被动目标感知、通信辅助、多感知模式、多源数据集成）。

Result: 建立了连接5G-Advanced与6G的3GPP架构与无线研究的统一技术视角，阐明了当前标准化选择、实现权衡及网络级感知面临的开放挑战。

Conclusion: ISAC从5G-Advanced到6G的演进为移动网络级感知能力奠定了标准化基础，但仍需在架构设计、技术实现和实际部署中解决多重挑战。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) extends mobile networks from information transfer toward perception of passive objects and environments. By exploiting propagation delay, Doppler, angle, and temporal variation, a mobile network can support detection and tracking while reusing licensed spectrum, infrastructure, and edge computing at network scale and under operator control. Unlike conventional radar, ISAC must additionally address multi-application access, heterogeneous sensing entities, uncertainty, privacy, trust, and integration with communication services. This article reviews the evolution of ISAC in 3GPP from 5G-Advanced to 6G. It explains Release-19 ISAC service requirements and channel models, the Release-20 sensing function architecture and reporting abstractions, and the 5G monostatic baseline for drone detection and tracking. It also examines 6G-native sensing, including passive object sensing, communication assistance, multiple sensing modes, and multi-source data integration. By connecting the evolving 3GPP architecture and radio studies across 5G-Advanced and 6G, this article provides a unified technical perspective on current standardization choices, their implementation tradeoffs, and the open challenges shaping network-grade sensing.

</details>


### [10] [On the Allocation of Transmit Power for Coordinated Spatial Reuse in IEEE 802.11bn Multi-Access Point Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11971)
*Francesc Wilhelmi,Boris Bellalta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于比例公平的Co-SR功率分配框架，证明了帕累托最优功率对至少使一个AP达到最大功率，将二维搜索简化为两个一维搜索，并通过仿真验证了联合评估两个AP链路对解锁空间复用增益的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEEE 802.11bn引入了Co-SR作为多AP协调方案，但缺乏有效的功率分配机制。现有方案未能充分利用空间复用增益，且未考虑AP间的公平性，因此需要设计一个低复杂度且公平的功率分配框架。

Method: 提出基于比例公平的功率分配框架，在每个TXOP基础上分配Co-SR传输功率。理论证明帕累托最优功率对至少保持一个AP在最大功率，从而将二维联合搜索简化为两个低成本的一维线性搜索。在离散速率系统中推导出精确的闭式表达式，并在Kom8ndor仿真器中进行数据包级仿真验证。

Result: 联合评估两个AP的链路是解锁Co-SR空间复用增益的关键；协调AP的公平最优功率严重依赖于速率建模方式（连续建模与离散MCS步进建模结果不同）；当前的11bn信令支持自私策略但不支持公平最优策略，后者需要额外的每TXOP信道报告。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了低复杂度的公平功率分配，能够在TXOP时限内完成计算。研究揭示了连续与离散速率建模对功率分配策略的重要影响，并指出了当前11bn标准需要增强信令机制以支持公平最优策略，为未来协议设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: IEEE 802.11bn (11bn) introduces Coordinated Spatial Reuse (Co-SR), a Multi-AP Coordination (MAPC) scheme in which two Access Points (APs) coordinate to control the transmit power for a simultaneous transmission. This letter introduces a Proportional Fairness (PF)-driven framework for allocating Co-SR transmit power on a per-Transmission Opportunity (TXOP) basis. We prove that any Pareto-optimal power pair keeps at least one AP at its maximum power, reducing the joint two-dimensional search to two cheap one-dimensional line searches that fit comfortably within TXOP timing, and show that this collapses to an exact closed-form expression in a real, discrete-rate system. We validate the resulting policies, together with a low-complexity selfish baseline, against a brute-force oracle and through packet-level simulations in Kom8ndor, an 11bn network simulator. Results show that jointly evaluating both APs' links is key to unlocking Co-SR's spatial reuse gains, that the coordinated AP's fairness-optimal power depends critically on whether the rate is modeled continuously or through real, discrete Modulation and Coding Scheme (MCS) steps, and that current 11bn signaling supports the selfish policy but not the fairness-optimal ones, which would need additional per-TXOP channel reporting.

</details>


### [11] [IPEK: Intelligent Priority-Aware Event-Based Trust with Asymmetric Knowledge for Resilient Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07532)
*İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出IPEK系统，通过引入事件感知和位置感知的非对称信任机制，有效检测VANETs中的智能攻击，在保持零误报率的同时实现超过75%的召回率和0.86以上的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VANET信任模型对交通事件采用同质化处理，无法检测智能攻击者利用低优先级事件积累声誉后在安全关键场景注入虚假数据的策略。现有方法在信任计算中忽略了事件严重性和位置关键性。

Method: 提出三方面贡献：1）形式化定义事件感知和位置感知的智能攻击模型；2）设计非对称本地信任机制，惩罚随事件和位置严重性缩放，奖励采用渐近模型；3）基于Yager组合规则改进Dempster-Shafer理论进行全局信任融合，结合顺序源可靠性排序和非对称风险强化机制。

Result: 在OMNeT++、Veins和SUMO仿真环境中，在15-35%攻击者密度下，IPEK在所有场景下保持0%误报率（无诚实车辆被错误撤销），召回率超过75%，F1分数超过0.86，性能显著优于MDT和TCEMD基准方法。

Conclusion: 将上下文感知集成到攻击建模和信任评估中，相比对称方法在对抗策略性攻击者方面具有显著优势，为VANETs安全提供了更可靠的信任管理方案。

Abstract: Vehicular Ad Hoc Networks (VANETs) are vulnerable to intelligent attackers who exploit the homogeneous treatment of traffic events in existing trust models. These attackers accumulate reputation by reporting correctly on low-priority events and then inject false data during safety-critical situations - a strategy that current approaches cannot detect because they ignore event severity and location criticality in trust calculations. This paper addresses this gap through three contributions. First, it introduces event-aware and location-aware intelligent attack models, which have not been formally defined or simulated in prior work. Second, it proposes an asymmetric local trust mechanism where penalties scale with event and location severity while rewards follow an asymptotic model, making trust difficult to regain after misuse. Third, it adapts Dempster-Shafer Theory for global trust fusion using Yager's combination rule - assigning conflicting evidence to uncertainty rather than forcing premature decisions - combined with sequential source-reliability ordering and an asymmetric risk accentuation mechanism. Simulations using OMNeT++, Veins, and SUMO compare the proposed system (IPEK) against MDT and TCEMD under attacker densities of 15-35 percent. IPEK maintained 0 percent False Positive Rate across all scenarios, meaning no honest vehicle was wrongly revoked, while sustaining Recall above 75 percent and F1-scores exceeding 0.86. These results demonstrate that integrating context-awareness into both attack modeling and trust evaluation significantly outperforms symmetric approaches against strategic adversaries.

</details>
