Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [189] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VAKRA基准测试，用于评估API和知识检索代理的综合推理能力，发现现有模型在复杂推理任务上表现显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将结构化API推理和文档集合推理能力分开评估，但企业环境中部署的代理需要同时具备这两种能力并进行联合推理。缺乏能够综合评估API交互、多跳推理和政策约束下推理能力的统一基准。

Method: 引入VAKRA基准，包含62个领域的8000多个可执行API，设计了三种难度递增的任务设置：多样化API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、以及带有自然语言工具使用政策约束的多源推理。通过实时重新执行预测的工具调用来验证正确性，支持多条有效路径。使用固定的ReAct框架隔离模型能力与代理架构，评估前沿模型和开源权重模型。

Result: 最佳模型在单跳端点式任务上仅达到70.4%准确率，在组合式API任务上降至50-51%。随着推理深度增加，性能下降超过50%。在政策约束问题上暴露严重失败（不可回答查询上低至2.4%）。失败分析显示问题集中在语言介导推理环节（实体消歧、跨源定位），而非工具调用机制本身。

Conclusion: 当前模型在企业级代理应用中存在显著推理瓶颈，特别是在实体消歧和跨源定位等语言介导推理任务上表现不佳，需要针对性地提升模型的综合推理能力以适应复杂的企业环境需求。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [190] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个利用检索增强生成(RAG)和大语言模型(LLM)自动化构建动态主逻辑(DML)模型并将其表示为知识图谱(KG-DML)的框架，成功应用于沸水堆低压冷却剂注入系统，实现了从技术文档到可执行功能模型的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态主逻辑(DML)为表示系统行为提供了分层框架，但其构建通常依赖专家对技术文档的人工解读，这限制了对复杂系统的可扩展性。因此需要开发自动化方法来构建DML模型，以提高构建效率和应对复杂系统的能力。

Method: 采用检索增强生成(RAG)和大语言模型(LLM)作为使能工具，通过目标检索在DML层级间进行模型构建，同时保持功能依赖关系和明确的逻辑关系。框架实现了从系统描述自动构建KG-DML模型，并支持诊断推理、安全评估、向上故障传播和向下依赖追踪。

Result: 在退役沸水堆的低压冷却剂注入系统上应用该框架，展示了在重复运行中的一致性重建能力。多层次验证方法评估了层级特定的精确度和召回率、逻辑门一致性以及整体结构完整性，证明了自动化KG-DML构建的可行性。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建能够将技术文档转换为可用于诊断和可靠性分析的可执行功能模型，为复杂系统的功能建模和安全性分析提供了一种可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


### [191] [Reproducing and Stress-Testing Two Approaches to LLM Reasoning Reliability: Test-Time Probability Aggregation and Logic-Representation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08514)
*Minhan Cho,Jimin Kweon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文独立复现并压力测试了两种提升LLM推理可靠性的方法（RPC和LCF），发现RPC在新领域的优势不显著，LCF的逻辑-有效性方向虽真实但较弱，两种方法的实际效果均不如原作者声称的那样稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理可靠性方法的评估均由原作者进行，缺乏独立复现和跨模型、跨领域的压力测试，且LCF未公开代码。验证这些可靠性声称具有重要意义。

Method: 独立复现RPC（推理时聚合token概率和自一致性）和LCF（训练投影器将隐藏状态分离为内容和逻辑，并编辑逻辑部分），扩展至文本转SQL、法律抽取、谬误识别和先例评分四个新领域，使用Qwen3-8B等四个7-8B模型进行测试，并直接探测LCF的表示。

Result: RPC在原作者数据上精确复现，但在四个新领域相对自一致性无显著优势（p>=0.28），在BIRD数据集上随着样本量增大优势消失甚至反转；LCF的逻辑-有效性方向真实但较弱（最佳子层可分性0.82 vs语义控制0.95），仅在Qwen3上有不显著的正向效果（p=0.56），在另外两个模型上显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 两种方法的效果均不如原报告稳健，强调了独立复现和跨领域压力测试的重要性，揭示了当前LLM推理可靠性方法的局限性。

Abstract: We independently reproduce two recent methods for making large language model (LLM) reasoning more reliable, and stress-test them across domains and models (RPC across four new task domains with Qwen3-8B, LCF across four 7-8B models). The first, RPC, aggregates token probabilities and self-consistency at inference; the second, LCF, trains projectors that split hidden states into "content" and "logic" and edits the logic part toward a valid region. Validating such reliability claims matters because the original evaluations are run by each method's own authors and were never independently reproduced or stress-tested across models and domains, and LCF shipped no public code. We re-run RPC's published-path aggregation and re-implement LCF's projector, contrastive, and intervention pipeline, then extend both to text-to-SQL, legal extraction, fallacy identification, and precedent grading, and probe LCF's representation directly. RPC reproduces the original grid exactly on the authors' released reasoning paths; on four new domains its edge over self-consistency is never significant (ties or small mixed differences, paired p >= 0.28), and on BIRD, the one domain where we vary the budget, the edge grows with K as predicted but its largest gap (+2.5 accuracy at K=32, p=0.16) reverses to -0.25 when we enlarge the sample to n=200. LCF's logic-validity direction is real but weak (0.82 separability at the single best sub-layer versus 0.95 for a semantic-attribute control); its one positive effect (Qwen3 $Δ$Prob) is not significant (p=0.56), while it significantly reduces $Δ$Prob on two of the other three models.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [An Event-Driven Cloud-Native Wearable Analytics Framework for Real-Time Clinical Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11402)
*Elias Grünewald,Daniil Cherepko,Linus Gustafsson,Jakob Möhler,Oskar Rabe,Paul Robin Reichelt,Constantin Stahl,Lukasz Sztukiewicz,Louis Agha-Mir-Salim,Felix Balzer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种事件驱动、云原生的可穿戴设备生理数据采集分析系统，实现了跨平台数据标准化（FHIR）、高效存储优化和实时分析，满足医疗合规要求，支持临床决策和科研应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级可穿戴设备为临床护理和研究提供了变革性机遇，但设备异构性、专有数据格式和严格监管要求阻碍了其集成应用，亟需一个可扩展、无厂商锁定的统一数据处理平台。

Method: 采用多层微服务架构和集群编排技术；通过跨平台移动应用利用原生健康框架实现数据采集；构建事件驱动的转换管道将原始测量映射到FHIR互操作标准；提出依赖感知的FHIR最小化方案减少存储开销；基于medallion lakehouse架构集成模块化数据分析和机器学习层，支持实时流处理到模型服务的完整ML生命周期。

Result: 系统在性能评估中实现了每秒50次完整摄取请求，中位响应时间低于8毫秒，满足实时患者监测的低延迟需求；开源实现符合监管合规标准（基于角色的访问控制、安全服务间通信）。

Conclusion: 该系统为在医疗机构环境中部署基于可穿戴设备的监测提供了稳健基础，能够有效支持临床决策支持和科研工作负载，推动了消费级可穿戴设备在临床护理和医学研究中的实际应用。

Abstract: Continuous physiological monitoring using consumer-grade wearables offers a transformative opportunity for clinical care and research, yet integration remains hindered by device heterogeneity, proprietary data formats, and strict regulatory requirements. We present an event-driven, cloud-native system designed to ingest, normalize, and analyze high-frequency vital signs from wearables at scale and without vendor lock-in. The system design proposes a multi-layered microservice architecture using cluster orchestration. Data acquisition is handled via a cross-platform mobile application that leverages native health frameworks, ensuring compatibility across fragmented device ecosystems. To address interoperability, we implement an event-driven transformation pipeline using stream processing engines and specialized services to map raw measurements to the FHIR standard for medical interoperability. Our novel dependency-aware FHIR minimization scheme reduces storage overhead while maintaining lossless resource reconstruction. Furthermore, the platform integrates a modular data analytics and machine learning layer based on a medallion lakehouse architecture, supporting the full machine learning lifecycle from real-time stream processing to model serving. Performance evaluation demonstrates that the ingestion pipeline sustains 50 full ingestion requests per second with median response times under 8 ms, satisfying the low-latency requirements for real-time patient monitoring. Our open-source implementation adheres to regulatory compliance standards through role-based access control and secure service-to-service communication, providing a robust foundation for deploying wearable-based monitoring in institutional healthcare settings for clinical decision support and research workloads.

</details>


### [193] [Descriptive Dispatch of Computational Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11524)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI/ML驱动的调度代理在多集群科学工作流中的可靠性，代理成功率达97.9%，描述性元数据将作业执行成功率从48%提升至87%，消除了架构不匹配问题，并使十个应用中五个的性能提升高达3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流在多集群环境中的运行面临着动态作业转换、调度和提交到异构集群的重大挑战。这些任务非常适合代理来完成，因为代理可以接收文本工作指令、准备作业规范并执行调度，但目前缺乏对此类代理可靠性的系统性评估。

Method: 通过432次运行评估调度代理的可靠性，测试了五种特征维度和四种提示样式的所有可能组合。同时测试了完整编排流程，包括提交、排队、匹配、评分、选择、转换和调度，并在多集群实验环境中验证了描述性元数据对作业执行的影响。

Result: 调度代理表现出高可靠性（97.9%成功率）。在220个提交作业中，描述性元数据将成功执行率从48%提升至87%，消除了架构不匹配问题，并使十个可测量应用中五个的性能提升了高达3.3倍。

Conclusion: AI/ML驱动的调度代理在多集群科学工作流编排中具有很高的可靠性和实用价值。引入描述性元数据能显著提高作业执行成功率，消除架构不匹配问题，并显著改善应用性能，证明了代理在异构集群环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Agents powered by AI/ML are becoming ingrained in orchestration. Dispatch of work is the task of receiving a request, transforming it for a workload manager, and successfully submitting it. Running scientific workflows across multi-cluster environments introduces substantial challenges of dynamic job transformation, dispatch, and submission to heterogeneous clusters. These tasks are well-suited to agents, which can receive textual instructions for work, prepare job specifications, and dispatch. In this work, we assess the reliability of a dispatch agent across 432 runs, testing all possible combinations of five feature dimensions across four prompt styles. The agent is highly reliable (97.9% success). We test a full orchestration to submit, queue, match, score, select, transform, and dispatch in a multi-cluster experiment. We find that descriptive metadata increases successful execution from 48% to 87% of 220 submitted jobs, eliminating architecture mismatch, and improving performance for five of ten measurable applications by up to 3.3x.

</details>


### [194] [User-Assisted Collaborative Distributed Inference for Efficient QoS-Aware Autoscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11840)
*Alfreds Lapkovskis,Ali Beikmohammadi,Sindri Magnússon,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合专用基础设施与用户贡献资源的协作式分布式AI推理系统，通过生成式马尔可夫模型优化资源分配，在保证服务质量的同时显著降低中心化基础设施成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI推理服务需求的增长，中心化服务基础设施成本不断攀升，迫切需要一种可扩展且经济高效的解决方案来应对日益增长的推理需求。

Method: 提出了一个协作式分布式推理系统框架，将专用基础设施与用户自愿贡献的资源相结合；开发了一个具有结构化时间分解的高维生成式马尔可夫模型，用于刻画用户、资源、任务和策略之间的随机动态交互，并支持任务调度和QoS感知的资源分配优化。

Result: 仿真实验表明，随着用户群体规模增长，分布式调度的优势日益凸显，在请求完成率和P99延迟方面表现优异，同时显著减少了专用资源消耗。

Conclusion: 用户辅助的协作式推理服务具有可行性，能够实现基础设施高效的自适应扩展，为AI推理服务提供了一种新的成本效益优化路径。

Abstract: Growing demand for artificial intelligence (AI) inference services requires scalable infrastructure, yet centralized serving costs rise with demand. We propose a collaborative distributed inference system combining dedicated infrastructure with resources contributed by service users. Dedicated resources provide baseline capacity for maintaining quality of service (QoS), while volunteered resources absorb increasing demand without proportional growth in centralized infrastructure. To capture stochastic and dynamic interactions among users, resources, tasks, and policies, we develop a high-dimensional generative Markov model with structured temporal factorization. The model supports simulation and provides a foundation for task scheduling and QoS-aware resource allocation optimization. We evaluate the system across user populations, resource capacities, and centralized and distributed scheduling policies. Simulations show that distributed scheduling becomes increasingly advantageous as the user population grows, improving request completion and P99 latency while substantially reducing dedicated resource consumption. These results demonstrate the feasibility of user-assisted collaborative inference for infrastructure-efficient autoscaling.

</details>


### [195] [Evaluating OpenMP Offloading for Intra-node Multi-GPU Programming across NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel Architectures: A 3D Heat Transfer Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11882)
*Ezhilmathi Krishnasamy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了OpenMP Offloading在单节点多GPU环境下求解3D热传导方程的性能表现，相比单GPU实现了约2倍（2 GPU）和4倍（4 GPU）的性能提升，并与原生GPU编程模型（CUDA、HIP、SYCL）进行了系统对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前超级计算机普遍配备多GPU节点，科学计算应用需要有效利用单节点内多个GPU。然而，在采用OpenMP Offloading进行跨架构GPU编程时，面临数据通信实现、编程模型选择和跨平台性能一致性等关键挑战，需要系统评估其性能表现与优化策略。

Method: 以3D热传导方程为基准测试，系统比较OpenMP Offloading与原生GPU编程模型（CUDA/HIP/SYCL）的性能差异。通过实现多种OpenMP Offloading方案，测试不同底层API（内存分配、同步/异步/点对点数据传输）对性能的影响，并在NVIDIA、AMD和Intel三种GPU平台上进行验证。

Result: OpenMP Offloading在多GPU配置下相比单GPU实现显著性能提升：2 GPU约2倍，4 GPU约4倍，该结论在三种GPU架构上均成立。通过优化内存传输策略（异步传输、P2P通信），OpenMP Offloading能够逼近原生编程模型性能。不同底层API选择对性能有显著影响，需针对具体硬件架构进行优化配置。

Conclusion: OpenMP Offloading作为跨平台GPU编程模型，在多GPU科学计算应用中具备实用性，能够在保持代码可移植性的同时获得显著性能收益。研究结果为科学计算应用在不同GPU架构间迁移提供了优化指导，证明了统一编程模型在异构计算环境中的价值。

Abstract: Currently, most supercomputers are equipped with GPUs from manufacturers such as NVIDIA, AMD, or Intel, which provide substantial parallelism and high throughput. It is common for a single compute node (intra-node) to host multiple GPUs, typically four or more. Therefore, effectively leveraging all these GPUs within a single compute node is essential for applications in scientific and engineering domains. However, several factors must be considered before utilizing these GPUs for scientific computing, including the implementation of data communication, the programming models available for use across these GPUs, and the level of performance that can be achieved with a single codebase across different GPU architectures and configurations within a single compute node. OpenMP Offloading is a prominent directive-based programming model that can be executed on all three GPU types: NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel. In this research, we present an analysis of the benefits and performance challenges of using OpenMP Offloading to address the 3D heat equations, which involve both primary computation, as well as halo computation and communication. We investigate how performance varies in relation to native GPU programming models--CUDA for NVIDIA, HIP for AMD, and SYCL for Intel. Furthermore, we demonstrate that OpenMP Offloading can achieve performance improvements of approximately 2x for 2 GPUs and around 4x for 4 GPUs when compared to single-GPU OpenMP Offloading implementations across all three GPU types. This analysis is conducted systematically through various OpenMP Offloading implementations that utilize different low-level APIs for memory allocation, memory transfer options (synchronous, asynchronous, and peer-to-peer), and other native GPU programming models such as CUDA (NVIDIA), HIP (AMD), and SYCL (Intel)

</details>


### [196] [Ready Cohorts: Bounding GPU Opportunity and Avoiding Host Round Trips in LLM-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12123)
*Josef Liyanjun Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代理服务中GPU执行的控制路径并发性，提出了量化模型来评估就绪队列边界，并证明设备内路由决策比传统的主机返回机制快1.19-2.39倍，为GPU代理控制建立了两个可测量的门槛条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理服务需要在模型调用和工具调用之间反复执行小型确定性转换（路由结果、更新状态、发出下一个效果）。研究者想知道这条控制路径何时能暴露足够的并发工作以利用GPU执行，以及当GPU计算的路由决策保留在设备上时会发生什么变化。这涉及到GPU批处理效率与控制流开销之间的权衡问题。

Method: 1. 形式化了就绪队列边界的概念，使用固定分区份额F、精确离线份额P*、局部上界U和在线实现份额A四个指标进行量化分析。
2. 在零服务时间、无限容量和相等相对启动截止期的假设下，设计了专门的动态规划算法来精确计算P*。
3. 使用固定的851会话公共轨迹面板进行稳态泊松重放实验。
4. 对比研究两种机制：传统的主机返回重派发机制与设备内路由决策机制。
5. 跨四种命名GPU放置方案测试36种配置，验证设备内路径的性能优势。
6. 通过14,557,440次批处理调用验证与主机预言机的一致性。

Result: 1. 在目标10万活跃会话、K=256、50ms启动截止期的条件下，得到F=30.19%、P*=43.00%、U=45.85%。
2. 精确打包恢复了81.83%在固定窗口边界处丢失的机会。
3. 设备内路径在所有36种配置中都比主机返回机制更快，行中位数比率范围为1.19x到2.39x。
4. 所有14,557,440次批处理调用都与单独实现的主机预言机匹配。
5. 固定嵌套设备图（不包含主机决策）在五种放置方案的60种配置中全部更慢。

Conclusion: 研究为GPU代理控制建立了两个可测量的门槛条件：截止期可行的队列供应和观察放置。设备内路由决策机制显著优于传统的主机返回重派发机制。要测量在线实现份额A、CPU置换和服务级别收益，需要连接的有限在线运行时环境。这为LLM代理服务在GPU上的高效执行提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: LLM-agent services repeatedly execute small deterministic transitions between model and tool calls: route an outcome, update state, and emit the next effect. We ask when this control path exposes enough concurrent work for GPU execution, and what changes when a GPU-computed route decision remains on device. We formalize the ready-cohort boundary using fixed-partition share F, exact offline share P*, local upper bound U, and online achieved share A. Under zero service time, unlimited capacity, and equal relative launch deadlines, a specialized dynamic program computes P* exactly. In a stationary Poisson replay of one pinned 851-session public trace panel, the primary condition at 100,000 target active sessions, K=256, and a 50 ms launch deadline gives F=30.19%, P*=43.00%, and U=45.85%. Exact packing recovers 81.83% of the opportunity lost at fixed window boundaries. The outcome-derived route key is a conditioning proxy, not proof of executable identity. A separate mechanism study keeps a GPU-computed binary decision on device instead of returning four bytes to the host and redispatching. Across four named GPU placements, the device-resident path is faster in all 36 configurations; within-placement row-median ratios range from 1.19x to 2.39x. Across both admissible mechanisms, all 14,557,440 tested batched invocations match a separately implemented host oracle. A fixed nested device graph that removes no host decision is slower in all 60 configurations across five placements. Together, the studies establish two measurable gates for GPU agent control: deadline-feasible cohort supply and observation placement. A joined finite online runtime is required to measure A, CPU displacement, and service-level benefit.

</details>


### [197] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了RoutePack，一个分层规划器，通过协调专家重路由和路由感知的数据打包，解决了MoE强化学习中的负载均衡问题，实现了8.85%-14.89%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE强化学习训练中存在两个相互耦合的负载均衡问题：序列组成决定密集注意力计算，token路由决定稀疏专家计算。单独优化任何一个都会将瓶颈转移到另一个问题上，需要协同优化。

Method: 提出RoutePack分层规划器：1）基于聚合路由需求在各MoE层独立放置专家；2）将样本打包到最小可行执行行中，使用投影EDP分片感知目标优化DP布局；3）通过并行种群退火搜索可行布局；4）采用状态一致性具象化保持逻辑top-k路由。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash模型上，专家重路由分别提升3.80%和10.50%的token吞吐量，路由感知打包额外提升4.86%和3.98%，总体吞吐量分别提升8.85%和14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过联合优化专家放置和数据打包，有效解决了MoE RL训练中的负载不平衡问题，在保持现有MoE内核兼容性的同时显著提升了训练吞吐量。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>
